Theorem 211.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any navier-stokes module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 339. The projective resolution has length at most 670 with Betti numbers bounded by 4.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 339 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the navier-stokes structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 670. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 4. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
